- **text-summarizer**: Summarise a chunk of text down to roughly `length` words using the agent's configured LLM provider. Input shape `{ text: string, length?: number }` on stdin, JSON; output shape `{ summary: string }` on stdout, JSON. Minimal: ~50 lines, no streaming, no retries — a deliberate baseline so the wiring is visible. For a production summariser, fork this and add retries, prompt-injection sanitisation, length validation, and per-model cost tracking. ([ChronoAIProject/Ornn](https://github.com/ChronoAIProject/Ornn/tree/HEAD/examples/text-summarizer) · ★20)
